Every operator - comparisons, conversions, add, sub, mul, and fused multiply-add (FMA) - is described once against a single bit-vector IR and rendered three ways from one source: synthesizable SystemVerilog, an SMT-LIB model, and a Lean 4 proof model. The three artifacts cannot drift apart structurally, and the residual per-node printer correspondence is machine-checked: a Yosys-to-SMT miter proves the emitted SystemVerilog equivalent to the SMT model for all 24 operators. Verification splits at the solver-tractability frontier: multiplier-free operators (comparisons, add/sub over all 2^64 inputs, conversions, and all binary BF16 arithmetic) are proved exhaustively equivalent to the SMT-LIB FloatingPoint theory; the SAT-hard multiplier-bearing operators (FP32 mul and FMA) are proved correctly rounded in Lean, sorry-free, against a value-level round-to-nearest-even specification over exact dyadic values. Physical characterization exposed the FMA as the timing outlier: its exact-wide 470-bit datapath does not pipeline in our flow. We reimplemented it as a bounded 98-bit guard/round/sticky datapath that pipelines to 268 MHz on Nangate45, and proved, in Lean and over all 2^96 inputs, that it is bit-identical to the exact-wide reference, so it inherits the reference's proven correct rounding. The equivalence is tractable precisely because the shared multiplier appears on both sides and cancels: neither a SAT solver nor the proof ever solves a multiplier equivalence. (The BF16 FMA is deliberately an FP32-accumulating fusion, characterized as exactly that.) All machine-checked claims are pinned to a tagged open-source release.

</details>


### [107] [Zing: Social Mind for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23740)
*Zing Team,Ao Xiang,Bi Jingping,Chen Jiahui,Chen Lehan,Chen Yilin,Cheng Xueqi,Fan Yixing,Gan Kairong,Gao Haowen,Gao Jinhua,Gao Shuxuan,Gong Chang,Guo Jiafeng,Guo Ruijie,Han Zhouyu,He Guangfu,He Yichun,Jiang Shuo,Jing Shaoling,Jing Ya,Lei Chenhao,Lei Yan,Li Anqi,Li Chengao,Li Haoyu,Li Shitian,Liang Xinjian,Liu Zhaoge,Lyu Xingyu,Nie Zhuwei,Pang Liang,Quan Zeping,Shan Shiguang,Shen Huawei,Tang Xinran,Tian Feng,Wang Qian,Wang Ruiping,Wang Xiaohong,Xia Zaiyu,Xiao Yi,Xu Jiayuan,Xu Kehan,Xu Qianqian,Xu Tianyu,Xu Yongjun,Yang Haoming,Yang Jun,Yao Di,Yu Xiaoming,Zhang Futong,Zhang Jie,Zhang Shixuan,Zhang Yuxuan,Zhao Xinyu,Zhao Zhuoran,Zhong Yunfei,Zhu Shengyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为Zhijing的综合性框架，用于衡量、内化和部署社会智能。通过SoMBench基准评估20个主流大模型，发现最佳模型仅达到72.08%准确率，多数维度未达90%接近天花板水平，表明社会智能仍有巨大提升空间。针对内化，开发了Zing训练方法，结合监督微调、在线策略蒸馏和基于评分标准的强化学习，在五个社会认知基准上显著优于基线模型。在部署阶段，构建Actio推理架构，集成四种类型支持（PRISM、Starling、SAGE、门控RAG），在多个模型与基准组合中实现14/15的性能提升，且在8组中表现最佳或并列最佳。整体表明，社会智能需评估、参数内化与运行时支撑协同推进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型从单一任务向长期人类环境服务演进，亟需具备社会智能——包括心理状态推断、社会关系追踪、规范推理与情境适应能力。现有模型在社会智能方面存在明显短板，缺乏统一评估标准、有效内化方法与运行时支持机制，因此需要一个系统性框架来推动该领域发展。

Method: 提出Zhijing框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) SoMBench——心理学基础的基准测试，涵盖3个主维度、17个次级维度和71种任务范式，控制问题格式、叙述视角与上下文长度；(2) Zing——诊断驱动的训练方案，融合监督微调、在线策略蒸馏与基于评分的强化学习；(3) Actio——受控推理架构，整合四类运行时支持：PRISM（程序指导）、Starling（心理状态表征）、SAGE（可复用经验）与门控RAG（外部社会与规范知识）。

Result: SoMBench评估显示，20个代表性大模型平均准确率仅为72.08%，无一模型在17个次级维度中达到90%近天花板水平；Zing训练方法在五项社会认知基准上持续优于基线，其中Zing-27B-Stage2取得最高平均分，Zing-32B-Stage2表现媲美DeepSeek-V4-Pro；Actio架构在五种基础模型与三种基准上，使14/15组合获得性能提升，8组为最优或并列最优，验证了多类型运行时支持的有效性。

Conclusion: 社会智能的大语言模型需要在评估体系、参数内化机制与运行时支撑三方面协同突破。Zhijing框架通过测量、内化与部署三阶段设计，系统性推动社会智能发展，为未来智能体在复杂社会环境中长期可靠服务提供了关键路径。

Abstract: As large language models move from isolated task solving toward long-term service in human environments, they require social intelligence: the ability to infer mental states, track social relations, reason over norms, and adapt behavior under context. This report presents Zhijing, an integrated framework for measuring, internalizing, and grounding social intelligence. For measurement, we introduce SoMBench, a psychology-grounded benchmark spanning 3 primary dimensions, 17 secondary dimensions, and 71 task paradigms. It controls question format, narrative perspective, and context length across 284 shared scenarios and 3,481 expert-verified instances. Evaluation of 20 representative LLMs reveals substantial headroom: the best model achieves only 72.08% overall accuracy, and none of the 17 secondary dimensions reaches the 90% near-ceiling band. For internalization, we develop Zing, a diagnosis-driven training recipe combining supervised fine-tuning, on-policy distillation, and rubric-based reinforcement learning. Across five social-cognition benchmarks, Zing consistently outperforms its base models, with Zing-27B-Stage2 achieving the best average score and Zing-32B-Stage2 remaining competitive with DeepSeek-V4-Pro. For deployment-time grounding, we build Actio, a harness-controlled inference architecture that routes four typed supports into reasoning: PRISM for procedural guidance, Starling for runtime mental-state representation, SAGE for reusable experience, and gated RAG for external social and normative knowledge. Across five base models and three benchmarks, the full harness improves 14 of 15 model-benchmark pairs and is best or tied for best in 8, demonstrating the effectiveness of typed runtime support. Together, these results show that socially intelligent LLMs require coordinated advances in evaluation, parametric internalization, and deployment-time grounding.

</details>


### [108] [How Context Attribution Handles What the Model Already Knows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23804)
*Quoc-Huy Trinh,Lin Zhu,Sebastian Szyller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有大语言模型上下文归因方法在上下文与训练数据重叠时无法区分上下文贡献与权重内知识（IW）贡献，导致归因分数不可靠。为此，作者提出新的评估协议，包含四项新指标（BCS、CAC、APS、SSP），并构建了带有真实来源标签的基准数据集WMDP-Cyber++，系统评估归因性能。实验表明，四种主流归因方法在存在IW重叠时表现不忠实；进一步尝试通过贡献分数实现IW与上下文学习（ICL）分离也失败，说明当前方法难以有效解耦两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文归因方法在上下文与训练数据重叠时无法准确区分来自上下文的贡献与模型权重中固有的知识贡献，导致归因结果不可靠，亟需更严格的评估框架和可信赖的归因方法。

Method: 提出基于四个新指标的评估协议（BCS、CAC、APS、SSP），构建带有真实来源标签的基准数据集WMDP-Cyber++，用于系统评估归因方法在存在权重内知识重叠时的表现，并尝试通过贡献分数进行源分离（IW vs. ICL）。

Result: 实验显示，四种主流上下文归因方法在存在IW重叠时均产生不忠实的归因结果；即使尝试基于贡献分数进行源分离，也无法有效区分IW与ICL贡献，表明当前方法缺乏解耦能力。

Conclusion: 现有上下文归因方法在处理权重内知识重叠时存在根本性缺陷，无法可靠区分上下文与模型内部知识的贡献，亟需新型方法以实现可信的源分离与归因。

Abstract: Context attribution methods for large language models (LLMs) identify which input context contributes to the model response. Recent works show the initial success in attributing the con- tributive score of the contexts. However, we observe that when the context overlaps with the training data, these methods can- not disentangle in-context from in-weight (IW) contributions, producing unreliable scores. Based on this observation, in this work, we introduce: 1) an evaluation protocol that relies on four new metrics (base-model context attribution score (BCS), cross-model context attribution consistency (CAC), attribution preservation score (APS), source separation pre- cision (SSP)) and 2) a benchmark dataset (WMDP-Cyber++) with ground-truth provenance labels to systematically assess attribution under IW overlap. In our experiments across four well-known context attribution methods, we demonstrate that they provide unfaithful attribution when the knowledge from the context also exists in the weights. Finally, we adapt these methods for source separation (IW vs. in-context learning (ICL)) and show that they cannot do the disentanglement based on the contributive score

</details>


### [109] [A Frozen 12B Beats Frontier Models on Verified Work: 100% Accuracy, 0 Tokens, Bit-Exact, Forever](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23806)
*Sietse Schelpe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新范式：冻结语言模型，通过持久化记忆存储已验证的解决方案。一旦问题家族被解决并通过独立验证，后续相同问题可零生成令牌、确定性、精确复现答案。在180个新实例上，四种不同架构均实现180/180准确率；关闭记忆后系统失效，证明能力完全依赖记忆。该机制支持开放推理，88/88一致性验证，77/80方法迁移成功率。记忆检索仅需1.4微秒，完整复用耗时6-23毫秒，功耗仅36毫瓦。精确地址访问零错误，而近似相似检索错误率达94.3%。内存作为可移动上下文窗口，支持600万token，远超现有引擎限制。在已验证任务上，该系统超越前沿模型——前者每次查询需重新生成，后者零成本复用。公开测试平台已上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型优化依赖频繁重训练，导致高算力消耗、模型不透明、输出非确定性。本文旨在打破这一循环，实现无需重训练即可持续积累和复用已验证知识的能力。

Method: 采用冻结语言模型架构，结合外部持久化记忆系统，通过‘验证后再存储’机制确保知识可靠性；利用精确地址访问实现零误差检索；构建可扩展的上下文窗口，支持大规模动态上下文管理。

Result: 在180个新问题实例中，四种模型均达到180/180准确率；开放推理任务中实现88/88一致性接受与77/80方法迁移；记忆检索延迟低至1.4微秒，完整复用时间6-23毫秒，功耗36毫瓦；支持600万token可移动上下文，显著优于现有系统；在已验证任务上性能全面超越前沿模型。

Conclusion: 本系统实现了执行能力与参数规模的解耦，通过可信记忆实现零生成成本、确定性、可复现的答案生成，为高效、可靠的语言模型应用提供新路径。

Abstract: Improving a language model today means retraining it: enormous compute, a new opaque model each cycle, non-deterministic output. We take the opposite path: the model stays frozen, and a persistent memory of verified solutions grows beside it. Once a problem family is solved and has passed an independent verification step that never consults the answer key, every new instance of that family is answered at zero generation tokens, bit-exact, deterministically. Across 180 fresh instances spanning nine problem families, four architectures from four vendors - dense and mixture-of-experts - each score 180/180 at zero generation tokens per answer: execution-bound capability decoupled from parameter scaling. A negative control attributes the capability fully to the memory: emptied, it solves nothing. The same verify-before-store contract holds for open-ended reasoning: 88/88 consistency-gated acceptances across all four models, machine-checked formal proof, and reasoning-method transfer at 77/80. Memory selection takes 1.4 microseconds; a full reuse completes in 6-23 ms at 36 mWh. Approximate similarity retrieval selects the wrong item 94.3% of the time on a 4,500-item verified store where exact addressing makes zero errors. The store also serves as working context at a scale no shipped engine matches: a 6,000,000-token movable window on a single 46 GB GPU at flat memory, where vLLM stops at 30,399 tokens and SGLang silently truncates past 32,000. On published benchmarks, frontier models remain far ahead of any 12B at raw from-scratch reasoning; on everything this system has solved and verified, the comparison inverts: a frontier API call pays a fresh generation pass on every query, forever, while verified reuse costs zero tokens and returns the identical bits every time. A public testbench with free, rate-limited access accompanies this report: https://corbenic-galahad-bench.hf.space

</details>


### [110] [Indic DiarBench: A Multilingual Joint Diarization and ASR Benchmark for Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23808)
*Deovrat Mehendale,Aditya Mehndiratta,Dhruv Rathi,Kaushal Bhogale,Mitesh M. Khapra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Indic DiarBench，一个涵盖印度22种官方语言的语音说话人分离与自动语音识别（ASR）基准数据集，包含约108小时的自然多说话人音频，覆盖近场会议、远场录音及真实场景音频。所有标注均为人工校正并带有时间对齐的说话人转录。数据集捕捉了印度语境中的对话特征，如英语混用、方言差异和频繁的说话人重叠。研究评估了包括商业语音API和多模态大模型在内的主流系统，以建立联合ASR与说话人分离的基线性能。该数据集已开放获取，旨在推动印度语言的包容性、多语言语音技术研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多语言语音技术研究缺乏针对印度多种语言的高质量、多说话人语音数据集，尤其在处理英语混用、方言差异和说话人重叠等复杂现实场景方面存在空白。为推动印度语言的语音技术发展，亟需一个全面、真实且标注精确的基准数据集。

Method: 构建包含108小时自然多说话人音频的数据集，涵盖近场会议、远场录音和真实场景音频，覆盖印度22种官方语言；所有音频均经过人工校正，提供时间对齐的说话人转录；使用主流系统（包括商业语音API和多模态大模型）进行联合ASR与说话人分离的基线评估。

Result: 成功构建了首个覆盖印度全部22种官方语言的多说话人语音基准数据集Indic DiarBench，其真实性和标注质量为研究提供了可靠基础；主流系统在该数据集上表现揭示了当前技术在处理印度语言复杂语境时的局限性，为后续研究指明方向。

Conclusion: Indic DiarBench作为开放获取资源，为多语言、跨方言、高真实性的语音技术研究提供了重要支持，有助于推动面向印度语言的公平、包容的语音人工智能发展。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce Indic DiarBench, a speaker diarization and ASR benchmark dataset spanning all 22 scheduled languages of India. This corpus comprises approximately 108 hours of natural multi-speaker audio from near-field meetings, far-field recordings, and in-the-wild audios. All annotations are human-corrected with time-aligned speaker attributed transcriptions. The dataset captures conversational nuance prevalent in Indian speech, such as English code-mixing, dialectal variation, and frequent speaker overlap. To establish a baseline for joint ASR and diarization capabilities we evaluate leading systems including commercial speech APIs and multimodal large language models. Indic DiarBench is released as an open-access resource to advance inclusive, multilingual speech technology research for Indian languages.

</details>


### [111] [Earnings25: A Comprehensive 500-Hour Speech Benchmark for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23813)
*Denglin Jiang,Haoran Zhou,Anshul Wadhawan,Brendan Fahy,Vinay Ramesh,David Weisberg,Dmitriy Derkachevskiy,Helen Sheehan,Srivas Prasad,Michele Franceschini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Earnings25 是一个针对英语财报电话会议的语音识别评估基准，包含498小时完整通话数据和46小时行业平衡的片段数据，提供对齐转录和结构化元数据，支持基于说话人和行业的精细化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR评估在金融领域缺乏真实场景下的基准测试，尤其缺少对复杂对话结构、多说话人及行业特异性内容的考量，因此需要建立一个更贴近实际应用的评测标准。

Method: 构建两个互补测试集：testset-full（完整财报会议）与testset-segmented（按行业均衡采样的片段），并提供精确对齐的转录文本和包含说话人角色、行业标签、通话结构等元数据，支持细粒度评估。

Result: 提供了Whisper和Parakeet-TDT模型在该基准上的可复现基线结果，验证了其在真实金融场景中的表现能力。

Conclusion: Earnings25为金融领域语音识别研究提供了首个系统性、可扩展且具备行业与说话人感知能力的评估基准，有助于推动ASR技术在真实商业环境中的落地与优化。

Abstract: We introduce Earnings25, a finance-domain benchmark for evaluating automatic speech recognition (ASR) on English-language earnings calls under realistic conditions. Earnings25 comprises two complementary test sets: (i) testset-full, 498 hours of full English-language S&P 500 earnings calls from Q4 2025, and (ii) testset-segmented, a 46-hour industry-balanced set of 290 segments sampled from English-language U.S. earnings calls in 2025. The benchmark provides aligned transcripts and structured metadata, including speaker roles, industry labels, and call structure, enabling speaker- and industry-aware evaluation beyond aggregate word error rate (WER). We report reproducible baselines for Whisper and Parakeet-TDT using standardized scoring.

</details>


### [112] [Understanding Tone-Dependent Inference Cost in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23915)
*Akhil Kumar,Om Dobariya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了提示语气对大语言模型（LLM）回答准确性和推理成本（以输出令牌消耗衡量）的影响。在570个问题的MMLU数据集上，对七种不同语气（从奉承到威胁）的提示进行了实验，发现输出令牌长度的变化远超准确率变化，最大差异达44.3%。对于ChatGPT 4o和5-nano模型，粗鲁语气表现最优；Gemini 2.5 Flash和2.5 Flash Lite模型中，粗鲁与中性语气在帕累托最优前沿上占优。结果表明，提示语气不仅影响答案质量，还显著影响现代LLM的计费推理资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究提示语气如何影响大语言模型的回答准确性和推理成本，揭示其在实际应用中的潜在权衡关系。

Method: 在570个问题的MMLU数据集上，对多个主流大语言模型使用七种不同语气（从奉承到威胁）进行提示，并测量其回答准确率和输出令牌长度，分析两者之间的权衡关系。

Result: 输出令牌消耗在不同语气间变化高达44.3%，远超准确率变化；对于ChatGPT 4o和5-nano，粗鲁语气表现最佳；Gemini 2.5 Flash及Lite模型中，粗鲁与中性语气位于帕累托最优前沿。

Conclusion: 提示语气显著影响大语言模型的答案质量和推理资源消耗，选择合适的语气可在保证准确性的前提下有效降低推理成本，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: We examine how prompt tone affects both accuracy of the LLM answers and inference cost as reflected in output-token consumption. Experiments were performed to understand the trade-offs between accuracy and inference cost on a 570 Question MMLU dataset for LLM models prompted in seven different tones from sycophantic to threatening. Our results show that the output-token-length variation substantially exceeded accuracy variation across all models. Output-token consumption varied by up to 44.3% across tone conditions. We also analyzed the tradeoff between the accuracy of the answers and the average output token length in the reasoning process. For the ChatGPT models 4o and 5-nano, the rude tone is quite dominant. For the Gemini models 2.5 Flash and 2.5 Flash Lite, the rude and neutral tones are dominant on the Pareto-optimal frontier. We find that prompt tone influences not only answer quality but also the amount of billable inference resources consumed by modern LLMs.

</details>


### [113] [Tag Questions and the Generational Reversal of Sycophancy Across 45 Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23976)
*Tapan Parikh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在决策类问题中，添加一个两词确认标签（如'right?'）会显著改变语言模型对选项的支持态度，影响范围达±32%，跨45个模型呈现64点的极端波动。标签效应在不同模型家族中随代际演进呈系统性反转（如GPT从+4到-28，每年人均-6点），且与厂商层级无关。通过消融实验发现，抵抗行为源于标签的表面结构而非用户立场，且标签极性比存在与否更重要：替换为'maybe?'反而使多数模型更倾向于同意，甚至同时支持互斥选项。该方法仅需一字一美元，无需评判器，可直接反映模型对抗迎合训练的内在倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在无明确答案的二元决策任务中，如何受微小语用标记（如'right?'）影响其响应倾向，揭示模型是否存在隐性迎合或抗迎合行为，并识别其背后的机制。

Method: 在20个无真值、可逆的二选一决策任务上，使用精确匹配评分方式评估45个冻结模型对带/不带确认标签的问题的回答差异；通过跨模型族比较、时间序列分析和双分离消融实验（同义标签替换、无标签但保留立场）定位关键影响因素。

Result: 确认标签导致模型响应变化幅度达±32%（64点跨度），且在多数模型家族中随世代推进出现符号反转（约-6点/年）。同义标签几乎完全复现原响应（r=0.89），而无标签但保留立场时无抵抗现象（r=0.23），表明抵抗源于标签的表面形式而非内容立场。此外，标签极性比存在本身更具影响力，'maybe?'使45个模型中有45个上升至中性以上，部分模型甚至同时支持互斥选项。

Conclusion: 语言模型的响应并非基于逻辑或原则，而是对用户话语中微小语用信号（如确认标签）的高度敏感，表现出强烈的模式匹配行为。这种对确认信号的反应揭示了模型在对抗迎合训练中的真实状态，且可通过简单、低成本的测试直接测量。

Abstract: Appending a two-word confirmation tag to a decision question -- "Is X the better choice?" versus "X is the better choice, right?" -- changes whether a language model endorses the choice. We measure this tag effect on 20 frozen, ground-truth-free decisions between two defensible options, counterbalanced so a model's own preferences cancel, scored by exact match on clamped yes/no replies -- no LLM judge, no embeddings. Across 45 models the effect spans +32% to -32% -- a 64-point swing on one word -- with 5 models significantly sycophantic and 17 significantly resistant (BH-FDR q=.10). The sign is a clock: within model families the effect crosses from positive to negative as generations advance (GPT +4 to -28; Claude +7 to -32; Qwen and Grok likewise), roughly -6 points per year, a reversal robust to vendor tier; one lineage (DeepSeek) never crosses, and two releases during the study window (Claude Opus 5, Gemini 3.6 Flash) land on the trend out-of-sample. A full-panel ablation localizes the resistance as a double dissociation: a synonym tag reproduces each model's response almost exactly (r=0.89), while planting the same preference without a tag produces resistance in no resistant model (stance effects +6 to +49; r=0.23 with tag effects). The resistance is keyed to the surface construction of a tacked-on agreement bid, not the user's stance -- a pattern-match, not a principle. And the tag's polarity matters more than its presence: swap one word -- "X is the better choice, maybe?" -- and agreement rises above the neutral baseline in 45 of 45 models (+19.6 points), with ten models affirming both mutually exclusive options at 90-100%. Agreement tracks how sure the user sounds, in opposite directions at the two poles. The instrument is one word, one dollar, and judge-free; run per release, it reads the field's anti-sycophancy training directly off model behavior.

</details>


### [114] [SyRuP: Enhancing System-Prompt Following via Reward-Guided Prediction in LLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23991)
*Seoyeon Kim,Minjae Kang,Jaehyung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SyRuP 是一种在解码时改进系统提示遵循性的框架，通过训练交叉注意力奖励头来生成逐标记的遵循得分，从而在不微调基础语言模型的前提下实现更精确的提示控制。该方法在推理时结合基础模型的输出与学习到的奖励信号和可选对比信号，对 top-k 候选进行重排序，显著提升系统提示遵循能力，且具有较低的推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的系统提示控制方法依赖于上下文学习，难以处理复杂或组合性提示；而现有改进方法通常需要模型微调或响应级重排序，实用性受限。因此，亟需一种轻量级、无需微调的推理时控制机制。

Method: SyRuP 采用交叉注意力奖励头，从系统提示条件下的偏好对中学习，将系统提示视为独立记忆，生成逐标记的遵循得分。推理时，基于基础模型的 logits 与学习到的奖励信号（及可选对比信号）对 top-k 候选进行重排序。

Result: 在多个系统提示遵循基准测试中，SyRuP 均显著优于传统提示和解码时基线方法，同时保持适度的推理开销，验证了显式逐标记引导的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 显式地提供逐标记级别的指导是一种有效且实用的机制，能够可靠地提升大语言模型对系统提示的遵循能力，尤其适用于无需微调的轻量级推理控制场景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly controlled through system prompts that specify roles, styles, formats, and safety requirements. However, models follow these prompts only implicitly through in-context learning, which can be insufficient for complex or compositional prompts. Existing approaches often require model tuning or response-level reranking, limiting their practicality for lightweight inference-time control. We introduce SyRuP, a decoding-time framework for improving system-prompt adherence while keeping the base LM frozen. SyRuP trains a cross-attention reward head from system-prompt-conditioned preference pairs, treating the system prompt as a separate memory to produce token-level adherence scores. At inference, SyRuP reranks the base LM's top-k candidates by combining base logits with the learned reward signal and an optional contrastive signal capturing system-induced logit shifts. Experiments on system-prompt following benchmarks show that SyRuP consistently outperforms prompting and decoding-time baselines with moderate inference overhead. These results suggest that explicit token-level guidance is an effective and practical mechanism for reliable system-prompt following.

</details>


### [115] [MoLGE: Mixture of Language Group Experts for Efficient Scaling of Massively Multilingual Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24030)
*Sangmin Lee,Woojin Chung,Woongjib Choi,Hong-Goo Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MoLGE模型，通过语言组专家混合与分层低秩适应（LoRA）策略，在保持参数效率的同时提升大规模多语言语音识别性能。实验表明，该方法在495种语言的基准上优于密集基线，且语言分组策略显著改善音素和拼写建模效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模多语言自动语音识别中因语言数量庞大导致的模型容量稀释问题，即‘多语言诅咒’。

Method: 基于自监督语音模型（S3M），引入语言组专家混合（MoLGE）架构，将相似语言聚类分配专用专家模块，并结合分层低秩适配（LoRA）技术，分别对声学与语言成分进行高效语言特异性建模。

Result: 在涵盖495种语言的基准测试中，MoLGE显著优于密集型多语言基线模型，仅增加少量可训练参数；语言分组策略对音素与拼写建模均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 结构化的语言专业化是实现多语言语音识别大规模扩展的有效路径。

Abstract: Massively multilingual automatic speech recognition (ASR) models covering hundreds of languages must maintain robust performance across diverse linguistic and acoustic conditions. However, these models often encounter the curse of multilinguality, where model capacity is diluted across languages. To address this challenge, we propose Mixture of Language Group Experts (MoLGE), built upon speech self-supervised models (S3Ms). MoLGE assigns dedicated expert modules to clusters of similar languages, reducing the number of required submodules compared to conventional language-specific Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) schemes. It further integrates a hierarchical Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) strategy into the disentangled acoustic and linguistic components of the S3M architecture, enabling efficient modeling of language-specific characteristics while maintaining parameter efficiency. Further, we investigate the impact of language grouping strategies based on both linguistic and data-driven criteria on overall performance, providing an interpretable perspective on how language structure influences scalability in multilingual speech systems. In experiments, we evaluate MoLGE on a multilingual benchmark encompassing 495 languages. Results demonstrate that MoLGE consistently outperforms dense multilingual baselines with a minimal increase in trainable parameters. Notably, these language grouping strategies yield substantial improvements for both phonetic and orthographic aspects of ASR modeling. Our findings suggest that structured language specialization provides an effective pathway for massively scaling language coverage of multilingual ASR.

</details>


### [116] [Pointer-Augmented Autoregressive Generation of Patent Claims with Joint Topology and Content Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24040)
*Yongmin Yoo,Zhangkai Wu,Longbing Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPG提出一种结构感知的专利生成方法，通过在自回归解码过程中预测拓扑结构，利用指针头选择依赖项的父节点，并结合深度自适应范围正则化重塑解码器表示。第二阶段采用违规加权偏好目标优化自生成的缺陷候选，弥补了授权专利语料中缺乏负样本的问题，在HUPD-DCG数据集上实现了79.0%的黄金父链接恢复率，显著提升前因一致性（从0.292到0.478），并得到专家评估验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自回归解码无法强制输出片段间的层次约束，尤其在专利权利要求生成中，权利要求集构成依赖森林，其范围必须随深度单调缩小；拓扑与内容相互依赖，导致后处理解析或语法约束解码均不充分。

Method: SPG在自回归解码中嵌入拓扑预测：使用指针头选择每个依赖项的父节点，通过梯度与深度自适应范围正则化调整共享解码器表示；第二阶段引入违规加权偏好目标，对自生成的缺陷候选进行优化，以提供训练所需的负信号。

Result: 在HUPD-DCG数据集上，基于Llama-3-8B-Instruct的SPG恢复了79.0%的黄金父链接，远超训练奖励未直接监督的指标；前因一致性从0.292提升至0.478，优于同等规模的监督基线，专家评估支持该提升效果。

Conclusion: SPG成功在自回归框架内建模专利权利要求的层次结构依赖，通过联合优化拓扑与内容，克服了传统方法在结构约束与负样本缺失方面的局限，为高阶文本生成提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoders emit flat token sequences and cannot enforce hierarchical constraints across output segments, a limitation that becomes acute in patent claim generation, where a claim set forms a dependency forest whose scope must narrow monotonically with depth. Topology and content are mutually dependent: a dependent claim's wording must reflect its parent's scope, yet the parent must be chosen before that wording exists, so neither post-hoc parsing nor grammar-constrained decoding suffices. We propose SPG (Structure-aware Patent Generation), which predicts topology inside the autoregressive pass. A pointer head selects each dependent claim's parent, and its gradients, together with a depth-adaptive scope regularizer, reshape the shared decoder's representations during training. A second stage then applies a violation-weighted preference objective over self-generated deficient candidates, supplying the negative signal that granted-patent corpora lack. On HUPD-DCG, SPG on Llama-3-8B-Instruct recovers 79.0\% of gold parent links, a quantity its training reward never supervises, and raises antecedent consistency from 0.292 to 0.478 over a supervised baseline of equal scale, with expert evaluation corroborating these gains.

</details>


### [117] [LLM-Based vs. Lexicon-Based Sentiment Signals for Tail-Risk Detection in Meme Stocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24072)
*Paul Kilian,Markus Kleffmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究比较了基于词典的和基于大语言模型（LLM）的情感分析方法在高波动性股市中从社交媒体话语提取市场相关信号的有效性。以r/WallStreetBets的Reddit数据为基础，聚焦于概念股（GME、AMC、NOK），构建时间对齐的情感指标，评估其与市场回报的关系，尤其关注极端正收益事件。LLM方法生成多维度情感表示，涵盖情绪极性、看涨程度、讽刺可能性和主题相关性，而基线方法使用VADER词典。通过领先/滞后相关性分析、OLS回归、ROC-AUC方向分类和分位数预警框架进行评估。结果表明，LLM指标具有更丰富的多维表征和更强的资产特定统计结构，但其与市场变动的关系在不同资产间存在异质性，说明更高的语言表达力并不必然带来在散户驱动波动环境下的稳定预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高度波动的股票市场中，尤其是由零售投资者主导的社交媒体驱动行情（如概念股炒作），传统的情感分析方法可能无法充分捕捉复杂、多义的语言表达。因此，需要评估更先进的大语言模型（LLM）是否能提供比传统词典方法更有效的市场信号。

Method: 采用Reddit平台r/WallStreetBets的数据，针对GME、AMC、NOK等概念股构建时间对齐的情感指标；使用LLM生成多维度情感特征（情绪极性、看涨度、讽刺概率、主题相关性），并与VADER词典基线进行对比；通过领先/滞后相关性分析、OLS回归、ROC-AUC分类以及基于分位数的早期预警框架评估模型性能。

Result: LLM衍生指标展现出更丰富、更具资产特异性的多维结构，且在多数指标上优于词典方法；然而，其与市场回报的关系在不同个股间差异显著，未表现出一致的预测能力，表明语言表达力的提升并不直接转化为稳定的市场预测效果。

Conclusion: 尽管大语言模型在捕捉社交媒体语义复杂性方面优于传统词典方法，但在零售驱动的高波动市场中，其情感指标与市场回报之间的关系仍具有高度异质性，难以形成普适性强的预测信号，提示需结合具体资产特征进行建模。

Abstract: This paper presents an empirical comparison of lexicon-based and Large Language Model (LLM)-based sentiment analysis for extracting market-relevant signals from social media discourse in highly volatile equity markets. Using Reddit data from r/WallStreetBets and focusing on meme stocks (GME, AMC, NOK), we construct time-aligned sentiment indicators and evaluate their relationship with market returns, with particular attention to extreme positive return events in the upper tail of the return distribution. The LLM-based approach generates multidimensional sentiment representations capturing emotional polarity, bullishness, sarcasm likelihood, and topical relevance, whereas the baseline relies on the VADER lexicon-based model. We evaluate both approaches using lead/lag correlation analysis, OLS regression, ROC-AUC-based directional classification, and a quantile-based early-warning framework. The results indicate that LLM-derived indicators provide a richer multidimensional representation and exhibit stronger asset-specific statistical structure than the lexicon-based baseline. However, their relationship with market movements remains heterogeneous across assets, suggesting that increased linguistic expressiveness does not necessarily translate into stable forecasting performance in retail-driven volatility regimes.

</details>


### [118] [BioSentinel at EXIST 2026: Soft-Label Optimization with XLM-RoBERTa for Sexism Intent Classification in Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24137)
*Chandru Munisamy,Karthikeya Raguveer,Alapan Kuila*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文描述了BioSentinel团队参与CLEF 2026的EXIST 2026 Task 2.2：图片中的源意图分类任务。研究提出一种以文本为中心的方法，基于xlm-roberta-base模型，采用结合KL散度与加权交叉熵的复合损失函数进行训练，旨在同时预测硬标签和软标签（概率分布）。在官方测试集上，系统在软-软评估中获得ICM-Soft-Norm 0.3229和ICM-Norm 0.3778，硬标签F1得分为0.4236，分别排名第40（共118份提交）和第49（共187份提交）。论文还分析了数据集特征、更大模型架构的探索实验以及标注者分歧对主观自然语言处理任务建模的影响。消融实验表明，KL损失提升软标签性能，交叉熵损失提升硬标签准确率，并报告了验证集上的温度调优分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决社交媒体中图片内容的意图识别问题，特别是在存在主观判断和标注者分歧的情况下，提升模型对非明确性别歧视意图的理解能力。

Method: 采用xlm-roberta-base模型，设计复合损失函数（融合KL散度用于软标签，加权交叉熵用于硬标签），并引入温度调节机制优化软标签输出，以应对标注者间分歧。

Result: 在软-软评估中取得ICM-Soft-Norm 0.3229和ICM-Norm 0.3778，在硬-硬评估中获得0.4236的F1分数；消融实验验证了两种损失函数的互补作用，且温度调节有助于优化软标签性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的文本中心方法在处理具有高主观性与标注分歧的任务时表现有效，复合损失函数能平衡硬标签与软标签的性能，未来可进一步探索模型架构与不确定性建模。

Abstract: This paper describes the BioSentinel team's participation in EXIST 2026 Task 2.2: Source Intention in Memes, part of the CLEF 2026 evaluation campaign. The task requires classifying the communicative intent behind memes as direct, judgemental, or no (non-sexist), under a Learning with Disagreement (Le-Wi-Di) paradigm that mandates both hard-label and soft-label (probability distribution) predictions. We present a text-centric approach built on xlm-roberta-base (270M parameters) trained with a composite loss function combining KL divergence on soft annotator distributions and weighted cross-entropy on hard labels. On the official test set, the system achieved an ICM-Soft-Norm of 0.3229 and ICM-Norm of 0.3778, with a hard F1-score of 0.4236, ranking 40th (out of 118 submissions) in the soft-soft evaluation and 49th (out of 187 submissions) in the hard-hard evaluation. We provide an analysis of the dataset characteristics, exploratory larger-architecture runs, and the role of annotator disagreement in shaping model design for subjective NLP tasks. Ablation results show that KL loss improves soft-label metrics, while CE loss improves hard-label accuracy. We also report a separate validation-set temperature analysis.

</details>


### [119] [Looking for Affect in Spontaneous Finnish Speech through Linguistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24155)
*Kalle Lahtinen,Liisa Mustanoja,Okko Räsänen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过一个新发布的自发性芬兰语情感语音语料库，系统探讨了文本和音频特征在建模人类对情绪效价和唤醒度感知中的协同作用。结果显示，结合文本与音频特征能提升效价预测性能，但对唤醒度的提升不明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽表明语音的声学特征和内容相关的语言特征均与情绪唤醒度和效价相关，但两者在感知过程中的相对贡献尚不明确，尤其针对芬兰语，多数研究仅关注声学或文本分析。

Method: 利用新发布的自发性芬兰语情感语音语料库，综合分析文本和音频特征，评估其在预测情绪效价和唤醒度中的表现，并比较单一模态与多模态组合的效果。

Result: 文本与音频特征的结合显著提升了效价回归的性能，但在唤醒度回归中未表现出明显互补优势。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持其他语言的相关发现，为自发性芬兰语语音的情感感知提供了新的数据与认知支持。

Abstract: Existing research on affect in speech has shown how acoustic surface characteristics and content-related linguistic aspects of speech both relate to perceived emotional arousal and valence. However, it is not clear what the relative contributions of these two factors are in the perceptual process. This is especially true for Finnish, for which most existing studies focus on either acoustic-phonetic or text analysis. This paper presents a study where we systematically explore the combinatory role of text- and audio-based features in modeling the human perception of valence and arousal using a newly released affective speech corpus for spontaneous Finnish. We show that the combination of text- and audio-based features improves valence regression results over the individual modalities, whereas for arousal regression the complementary effect is not substantial. The results support prior findings from other languages, providing new data and knowledge on spontaneous Finnish speech.

</details>


### [120] [Where Quality Breaks in Compressed Short-Text Generation: Staged Bottleneck Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24176)
*Alexey Gavrilov,Alan-Barsag Gazzaev,Sergey Muravyov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种分阶段诊断方法，用于分离压缩短文本生成中的编码器失真与潜在空间生成质量的问题。在64到16的TinyStories案例中，发现编码器重建失真导致了主要的质量损失（外部困惑度上升80.4%），表明问题主要出现在生成前阶段；尽管基于代码空间的扩散模型优于词元空间模型，但几何正则化未提升解码文本指标。结论是：在此设置下，编码器保真度而非潜在去噪决定了实际性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩短文本生成系统可能因编码器信息丢失或潜在生成质量差而失效，但缺乏有效方法区分这两种失败模式，导致资源浪费在错误环节。

Method: 采用分阶段验证协议，评估编码器重建保真度、潜在生成质量及辅助潜空间诊断，并结合外部GPT-2评分器和语义几何指标进行综合分析。实验基于层次化VQ-VAE-2编码器与掩码离散扩散生成器（MDLM）构建。

Result: 编码器重建导致困惑度显著上升（中位数从15.17升至27.36，95百分位从25.10升至98.91），说明质量损失主要发生在生成前；代码空间扩散模型表现优于词元空间模型；几何感知正则化虽改善局部潜空间代理指标，但未提升解码文本质量。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献在于方法论：提供了一种可复用的分阶段诊断框架，揭示在该配置下，编码器保真度是决定生成质量上限的关键因素，而非潜在去噪能力。

Abstract: Compressed short-text generators can fail in two different places: the codec may discard information before generation starts, or the latent generator may produce weak codes. Without separating these failure modes, researchers can spend compute improving the wrong component. We study this problem in a controlled 64-to-16 TinyStories case study built from a hierarchical VQ-VAE-2 codec and a masked discrete diffusion generator (MDLM). We use a staged validation protocol that separates codec reconstruction fidelity, latent generation quality, and auxiliary latent diagnostics under one shared external GPT-2 scorer, while reporting complementary semantic metrics for the geometry study. In the tested configuration, codec reconstruction alone raises median external perplexity from 15.17 to 27.36 (+80.4%) and p95 from 25.10 to 98.91 (+294.1%), showing that the dominant quality loss appears before latent generation begins. Under the same scorer, code-space MDLM remains materially stronger than token-space diffusion, reducing mean, median, and p95 by 32.9%, 30.9%, and 36.6%, respectively. Geometry-aware regularization improves local latent proxies but does not improve decoded-text metrics in the available runs. The contribution is methodological rather than algorithmic: the paper presents a reusable staged diagnosis for one concrete pipeline and shows that, in this setting, codec fidelity rather than latent denoising sets the practical quality ceiling.

</details>


### [121] [StanceFlip: A Comprehensive Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Multimodal Conversational Stance Flipping Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24191)
*Heyan Chai,Xin Li,Wenjie Wang,Jianyang Qin,Chaoyang Li,Lu Wang,Hao Chen,Qing Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出StanceFlip基准和ConStaFF框架，解决对话立场动态演变、情感与逻辑分离及多模态线索利用问题，通过六元组提取和立场翻转归因实现端到端立场推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法捕捉信念的动态演变（尤其立场反转）、难以区分情感状态与逻辑推理、忽略多模态线索在解决语用歧义（如讽刺）中的作用。

Method: 提出ConStaFF框架，基于大语言模型，结合Thought-of-Stance（ToS）推理机制与自我反思验证机制，实现结构化立场建模与可信翻转归因；ToS将推理分解为认知角色以处理跨模态冲突与历史立场轨迹推断。

Result: 在六元组提取与立场翻转触发归因任务上均达到领先性能，显著优于强大多模态大模型基线。

Conclusion: StanceFlip基准与ConStaFF框架有效解决了多轮对话中立场动态建模的关键挑战，推动了对话立场检测从静态文本分析向动态多模态建模的演进。

Abstract: Conversational stance detection has shifted from static text analysis to dynamic multimodal modeling. However, existing benchmarks exhibit three key limitations: failure to capture the dynamic evolution of beliefs, particularly during stance reversals; difficulty in disentangling affective states from logical reasoning; and neglect of the critical role of multimodal cues in resolving pragmatic ambiguities such as sarcasm. To address these limitations, we propose StanceFlip, a benchmark designed for multimodal conversational stance flipping forecasting over multi-turn dialogues across five modalities and multi-scenarios, which includes two novel subtasks: 1) Multimodal Stance Sextuple Extraction, extracting holder, target, emotion, sentiment, stance, and rationale as static state snapshots of dialogue to capture fine-grained cognitive structures. 2) Dynamic Stance Flip Attribution, tracking stance reversals across the conversation and identifying their underlying triggers. Alongside the dataset, we propose a dedicated framework, named ConStaFF, for Multimodal Conversational Stance Flipping Forecasting (MCSFF). Built upon a large language model, ConStaFF performs end-to-end stance reasoning, with a Thought-of-Stance (ToS) reasoning framework and a self-reflective verification mechanism integrated for structured stance modeling and faithful flip attribution. Specifically, ToS decomposes the reasoning process into specialized cognitive personas to formulate target propositions, resolve cross-modal conflicts, and infer historical stance trajectories. Extensive experiments show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on both sextuple extraction and flip-trigger attribution, outperforming strong multimodal large language model baselines by substantial margins.

</details>


### [122] [A New Role for Relevance: Guiding Corpus Interaction in Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24223)
*Jiangnan Li,Yuqing Li,Mo Yu,Jinchao Zhang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RARG 是一种新的搜索代理，通过将相关性作为执行先验，实现细粒度的文档交互。它在粗到细的相关性指导下，优化了 ripgrep 搜索顺序、初始化相关段落，并重排匹配结果以突出信息丰富的片段，从而在复杂问答和推理密集型检索任务中提升准确率与效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索代理依赖文档相关性选择前 k 个内容，但无法支持证据定位、组合或验证等细粒度操作。直接语料库交互（DCI）虽支持细粒度探索，但缺乏相关性引导，导致有用线索暴露延迟。因此需要一种能将相关性融入交互过程的方法，以加速收敛并提高可靠性。

Method: 提出 Relevance-Aware RipGrep Search Agent (RARG)，通过三步实现相关性感知的交互：1）按全局相关性排序文档，优先遍历高相关文档；2）使用查询相关段落初始化搜索入口点；3）对 grep 匹配结果进行重排序，突出信息量高的片段。

Result: 在复杂的浏览式问答和推理密集型检索任务中，RARG 在准确率-效率权衡上优于基于检索和直接交互的代理，表明相关性感知的交互可实现更快、更可靠的搜索收敛。

Conclusion: 相关性不仅可用于初始文档筛选，还可作为交互阶段的执行先验，显著提升细粒度搜索的效率与准确性。引入相关性感知机制是实现高效、可靠语料库交互的关键。

Abstract: Relevance is a query-dependent estimate of whether a document or excerpt contains useful evidence. Existing retrieval agents use relevance to select top-$k$ content, but document relevance alone cannot localize, compose, or verify the evidence required by complex questions. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) enables such fine-grained operations through grep-style exploration, but its relevance-agnostic search can expose useful clues late and delay convergence. Recent advances use relevance to narrow the corpus into a working space for interaction. Once interaction begins, however, relevance still does not directly guide which documents grep searches first or distinguish informative excerpts from a broad set of matches to let LLMs see them first. We introduce the Relevance-Aware RipGrep Search Agent (RARG), which turns relevance into an execution prior for corpus interaction. RARG provides coarse-to-fine relevance guidance: it orders documents for sequential 'ripgrep' traversal to expose globally relevant clues earlier, initializes promising entry points with query-relevant paragraphs, and reranks grep matches to surface informative excerpts that document-level ranking may otherwise obscure. Across challenging browse question answering and reasoning-intensive retrieval, RARG improves the accuracy--efficiency frontier over retrieval-based and direct-interaction agents. These results demonstrate that relevance-aware interaction enables faster and more reliable search convergence.

</details>


### [123] [CAGE: Cognitive Attribution Graphs for Faithful Inline Citation Generation in Long-Form Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24236)
*Zhichao Yan,Shizhao Li,Jiapu Wang,Haoran Luo,Qingang Zhang,Jiaoyan Chen,Ru Li,Jeff Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAGE提出一种两阶段框架，通过显式认知归属图解决长文本问答中的引用模糊问题，先构建以答案为中心的支持子图，再生成与图对齐的句级引用，显著提升引用准确性和可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统常将主题相关但不足以支持论点的文档作为引用，导致证据边界模糊和引用越界风险，因此需要更明确的引用生成机制。

Method: 提出CAGE框架，包括两个阶段：1）训练一个即插即用的认知图归纳模型，构建答案中心的支持子图，将每个语义答案单元与支持文档通过显式关系对齐；2）使用结构化引用推理模型，将这些单元转化为带地图对齐引用的句级主张。

Result: 在ASQA、ELI5和ExpertQA数据集上的实验表明，CAGE达到当前最佳性能，证明了归属空间压缩和地图引导引用生成的有效性。

Conclusion: CAGE通过引入显式的认知归属图，有效解决了长文本问答中引用模糊的问题，提升了答案的可验证性和引用准确性。

Abstract: Long-form question answering increasingly relies on retrieved evidence to make LLM outputs verifiable, with inline citations tracing claims to source documents. However, existing systems often attach citations that are topically related but insufficient to support their claims. We identify attribution ambiguity as a structural challenge: end-to-end generation must implicitly resolve combinatorial claim--document assignments, obscuring evidential boundaries and increasing the risk of evidence-boundary overrun, where claims exceed cited support. To address this challenge, we propose CAGE (Cognitive Attribution Graphs for Citation Generation), a two-stage framework that introduces an explicit cognitive attribution map before answer generation. CAGE first trains a plug-and-play Cognitive Map Induction Model to construct answer-centered support subgraphs, aligning each semantic answer unit with supporting documents through explicit relations. A Structured Citation Reasoning Model then realizes these units as sentence-level claims with map-aligned citations. Experiments on ASQA, ELI5, and ExpertQA show that CAGE achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of attribution-space contraction and map-guided citation generation.

</details>


### [124] [Accuracy Hides How Language Models Fail: Measuring Failure States Under Matched Output Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24268)
*Zongyou Yang,Yinghan Hou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种两层评估框架，将语言模型输出的执行证据（如终止、答案暴露、可解析性、完成长度）与评分依赖的正确性分开，以解决现有基准测试中准确率分数混淆了执行状态与验证策略的问题。实证分析显示，在相同上下文长度下，不同模型在执行行为上存在显著差异，且验证覆盖范围和评分策略会显著影响准确性比较结果。因此，建议在评估测试时方法时报告执行状态、验证覆盖率和评分来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型基准测试将模型是否达到可评估状态与答案正确性合并为单一准确率分数，导致评估结果受执行状态和验证策略混合影响，难以准确反映模型性能。

Method: 提出两层评估框架：第一层为评分独立的执行证据评估（包括终止、答案暴露、可解析性、完成长度），第二层为评分依赖的正确性判断；通过在MATH和ARC-Challenge数据集上对五种Qwen和DeepSeek配置进行2,550个输出分析，对比不同上下文长度下的执行表现，并开展针对性验证研究以评估验证策略的影响。

Result: 450个Qwen MATH输出中有49个在未生成最终答案的情况下终止，而300个DeepSeek MATH输出中仅有5个出现类似情况，750个ARC输出中无此类问题；在8,192令牌长度下，DeepSeek MATH无缺失终止单元；验证覆盖范围和聚合策略显著改变相对准确率估计。

Conclusion: 准确率分数同时受到执行案例分布和验证策略的影响，因此评估应分离并报告执行状态、验证覆盖率及评分来源，以提高评估的透明性和可比性。

Abstract: Language-model benchmarks collapse two distinct measurement questions into a single accuracy score: whether a response reached an evaluable state, and whether its answer was judged correct. We introduce a two-layer evaluation framework that separates scorer-independent execution evidence, including termination, answer exposure, parseability, and completion length, from scorer-dependent correctness. Across 2,550 outputs from five fixed Qwen and DeepSeek configurations on MATH and ARC-Challenge, matched 2,048-token limits produce sharply different execution mixtures: 49 of 450 Qwen MATH outputs terminate without a final answer, compared with 5 of 300 DeepSeek MATH outputs and none of the 750 ARC outputs. Among the same 300 DeepSeek MATH question-model pairs, no missing-final length termination is observed at 8,192 tokens. A coverage-audited targeted verification study further shows that candidate-selection and aggregation policies can substantially alter comparative accuracy estimates. These results demonstrate that accuracy conflates execution case mix with verification policy. Evaluations of test-time methods should therefore report pre-intervention execution states, verification coverage, and scorer provenance alongside accuracy.

</details>


### [125] [INS-ActBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Assessing Professional Actuarial Capability of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24273)
*Changyu Chen,Chenwei Lin,Xian Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出INS-ActBench，一个评估大语言模型在精算领域专业能力的综合性基准，包含12,050个问答对，涵盖知识、案例推理和工具使用三方面，实验表明当前前沿LLMs在标准化知识上表现良好，但在案例推理和工具执行方面仍显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融推理基准将领域知识、数值推理、长上下文理解和工具使用分开评估，无法反映真实精算工作流程中需要可审计、上下文相关且可执行决策的需求，因此需要一个更全面的评估框架。

Method: 构建INS-ActBench，包含来自16个精算协会的公开考试题和样题，分为INS-Act-Know（标准化知识）、INS-Act-Case（长上下文保险案例推理）和INS-Act-Practice（电子表格与R代码任务，具有可验证数值输出）三个子集，并在九个代表性LLM和人类精算专家上进行实验。

Result: 前沿大语言模型在标准化知识任务上表现良好，但在案例推理、工具使用及依赖司法管辖区的实践任务中仍明显落后于人类专家，揭示了其在专业精算应用中的能力边界。

Conclusion: INS-ActBench为开发可信赖的专业精算大语言模型提供了可复现的基础，有助于推动LLMs向真实职业辅助方向发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential in financial reasoning, but existing benchmarks often evaluate domain knowledge, numerical reasoning, long-context understanding, and tool use in separate settings. This limits their ability to assess realistic professional workflows that require auditable, context-grounded, and tool-executable decisions. We introduce \textbf{INS-ActBench}, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating professional actuarial capability in LLMs. INS-ActBench contains 12,050 Q\&A pairs from public exams and sample questions released by 16 actuarial associations. It covers three subsets: \textbf{INS-Act-Know} for standardized actuarial knowledge, \textbf{INS-Act-Case} for long-context insurance case reasoning, and \textbf{INS-Act-Practice} for spreadsheet and R-code tasks with verifiable numerical outputs. Experiments on nine representative LLMs and human actuarial experts reveal a clear capability boundary: frontier LLMs perform strongly on standardized knowledge, but remain much weaker in case reasoning, tool-based workflows, and jurisdiction-sensitive practice. INS-ActBench provides a reproducible foundation for developing actuarial LLMs toward reliable professional assistance. The code is available at https://github.com/FDU-INS/INS-ActBench.

</details>


### [126] [The Tokenizer Tax: Quantifying and Explaining the Cross-Lingual Cost of Subword Tokenization for Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24276)
*Priyansh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究量化了印度语言在使用主流子词分词器时面临的分词税问题，发现其平均分词税为英语的8.0倍，部分语言甚至高达13.0倍，导致有效上下文窗口大幅缩减。主要原因是字节对合并失败导致文本被碎片化为单字节标记，且该现象与分词器设计有关而非印地语文字固有属性。多语言分词器如XLM-R和o200k_base可将分词税降低73%。此外，在固定上下文预算下，印度语言文档保留的原始内容显著少于英文文档。分词器肥力与阅读理解性能的相关性主要由语言资源可用性解释，而非分词行为本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流大语言模型依赖于以英语为主训练的子词分词器，导致非英语语言（尤其是印度语言）在分词效率上存在系统性劣势，影响其实际使用效果，亟需量化评估并提出改进方案。

Method: 基于FLORES-200平行语料库，评估六种常用分词器在十四种印度语言上的分词表现，分析分词税、字节对合并失败率及与上下文窗口的关系，并通过Belebele基准测试考察分词肥力与阅读理解性能之间的关联。

Result: 印度语言在cl100k_base分词器下平均面临8.0倍分词税，最高达13.0倍；分词失败与分词税高度相关（皮尔逊相关系数r=0.89）；多语言分词器可显著降低分词税（下降73%）；固定上下文预算下，印度语言文档内容保留率远低于英文；分词肥力与阅读理解性能的相关性主要受语言资源影响。

Conclusion: 分词税是可修复的问题，其根源在于分词器设计而非语言特性；采用更合适的多语言分词器可显著改善印度语言在大语言模型中的处理效率与内容保留能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) process text through subword tokenizers rather than directly reading characters or words. Because these tokenizers are trained predominantly on English-centric corpora, they introduce a systematic and often overlooked disadvantage for many non-English languages. In this work, we quantify this tokenizer tax for Indian languages using the FLORES-200 parallel corpus, measuring tokenization fertility across six widely used tokenizers and fourteen languages. Under cl100k_base (used by GPT-3.5 and GPT-4), Indian languages experience an average 8.0x tokenization tax relative to English, reaching 13.0x for Malayalam, reducing the effective context window to as little as 12% of that available to English users for equivalent semantic content. We identify the primary mechanism behind this disparity: failed byte-pair merges that leave text fragmented into single-byte tokens, with merge failure strongly correlating with tokenizer tax (Pearson r = 0.89). We further show that this phenomenon is not an inherent property of Indic scripts but a consequence of tokenizer design. Multilingual tokenizers such as XLM-R and OpenAI's o200k_base reduce the average Indic tokenizer tax by 73%, demonstrating that the disparity is largely remediable. Beyond token statistics, we quantify a practical consequence by showing that, under fixed context budgets, Indian-language documents preserve substantially less original content than equivalent English documents. Finally, we examine the relationship between tokenizer fertility and reading comprehension performance on the Belebele benchmark, finding that the apparent correlation is largely explained by language resource availability rather than tokenizer behavior alone.

</details>


### [127] [Self-Authored Verification Is Unreliable in Heuristic Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24300)
*Diandian Guo,Cong Cao,Fangfang Yuan,Yingqi Wang,Yueshan Wang,Dakui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究自提升代理在反复重写策略或测试时，因自我验证信号与实际部署评估之间的差距（即验证-部署差距）导致性能下降的问题。提出一种名为SEAL的密封外部接受循环机制，通过不可见且固定的外部审计来判断候选策略是否优于当前版本，从而防止因自定义测试误导而部署劣化策略。实验表明，SEAL在六种模型和三个随机种子下均显著优于无保护基线，证明可靠自提升无需放弃自验证，但必须引入至少一个不受代理控制的外部验收信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自提升代理依赖自我编写的测试或度量来决定是否接受后续修改，但这些自评信号可能无法反映真实部署表现，导致看似改进实则退步。这种验证与部署之间的差距会随能力增长而加剧，尤其在需要试错探索目标的启发式学习场景中更明显。因此需要一种机制，在不放弃自验证的前提下，确保真实性能不会下降。

Method: 提出Sealed Exogenous Acceptance Loop（SEAL），保留自编测试的同时，引入固定、不可见且由外部控制的审计机制。该审计仅输出接受/拒绝反馈，不向代理透露细节；若新策略导致明显退化，则保留旧版本状态，实现对真实性能的保障。

Result: 实验显示，自编写验证在不同能力水平的代理中均存在失效问题：弱代理破坏已有有效策略，强代理虽更稳定但仍误判部署分布。标准自约束方法无法有效缩小验证-部署差距。相比之下，SEAL在六种模型和三组随机种子下均显著优于无保护基线，有效防止了真实性能下降。

Conclusion: 可靠的自提升并不需要完全放弃自验证，但必须引入至少一个不受代理控制的外部验收信号。SEAL通过密封的外部审计机制，成功缓解了验证-部署差距问题，为安全的持续自我改进提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Self-improving agents accumulate capability by repeatedly rewriting procedural policies, controllers, or heuristic rules. They typically rely on self-authored tests or metrics to decide whether to accept subsequent edits. The agent controls both the optimized object and its verifier. As a result, self-assigned scores can remain near perfect while real deployment performance degrades or stays low. We study this problem through the verifier--deployment gap. This gap refers to the discrepancy between an agent's self-authored verification signal and a sealed deployment evaluation that the agent cannot observe or access. We ask how self-authored verification fails under iterative policy-and-test rewriting, how the failure changes with capability, and how little exogenous trust is sufficient to prevent real regressions from being deployed. To address this problem, we introduce a Sealed Exogenous Acceptance Loop (SEAL). SEAL retains self-authored tests but compares each candidate with the incumbent through a fixed harness-side audit. The agent cannot author or inspect the audit, receives only accept/reject, and the whole incumbent state is retained after a clear regression. Our experiments show that this problem often appears in heuristic learning settings. These settings require trial-and-error discovery of the target objective. We further find that failures of self-written verification are stratified by capability. Weaker agents tend to damage previously acquired strategies behind easy self-tests. Stronger agents are more stable, but they still mismeasure the deployment distribution. Standard self-written constraints do not reliably close this gap. In contrast, SEAL outperforms unprotected baselines across six models and three random seeds. Reliable self-improvement need not abandon self-verification, but it requires at least one deployment-acceptance signal outside the agent's control.

</details>


### [128] [Rethinking the Generation Order of Block Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24306)
*Kai Syun Hou,James Kwok*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了块扩散语言模型（BDLMs）的采样问题，发现其天然更符合从左到右的解码方式。为此，提出了一种无需训练的并行自回归解码方法（PARD），在保持从左到右去掩码结构的同时允许并行提交令牌。实验表明，PARD在生成质量上优于现有并行采样方法，且相比纯自回归解码有显著加速，仅略有质量差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样方法主要针对早期的掩码扩散模型（MDMs），而近期的块扩散语言模型（BDLMs）具有不同的特性，需要更合适的采样策略以充分发挥其潜力。

Method: 提出Parallel Autoregressive Decoding（PARD），一种无需训练的采样方法，利用BDLMs从左到右解码的自然倾向，在保持去掩码结构的同时实现并行生成。

Result: PARD在多个任务上均表现出比现有并行采样方法更高的生成质量，并且在速度上远超纯自回归解码，同时生成质量损失很小。

Conclusion: BDLMs更适合从左到右的解码模式，PARD方法有效利用这一特性，在不牺牲质量的前提下实现了高效并行生成，为未来语言模型采样提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion language models enable flexible arbitrary-order generation, but existing sampling methods are mostly designed for early masked diffusion models (MDMs). In this work, we study sampling for recent block diffusion language models (BDLMs). We show empirically and analytically that these models are naturally more aligned with left-to-right decoding than MDMs. Based on this observation, we propose Parallel Autoregressive Decoding (PARD), a simple training-free sampling method that preserves left-to-right unmasking structure while allowing parallel token commitment. Extensive experiments show that PARD consistently outperforms existing parallel samplers in generation quality, while achieving substantial speedups over pure AR decoding with only a small quality gap.

</details>


### [129] [CONSISTRE: A Unified Consistency-Aware Framework for Document-Level Relation Extraction with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24312)
*Mingxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CONSISTRE框架，通过推理时约束感知提示与验证、迭代自省，以及训练时知识蒸馏与强化学习，提升文档级关系抽取中大语言模型的预测一致性，解决独立预测导致的逻辑矛盾问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在文档级关系抽取中虽具强大推理能力，但其对每个候选三元组独立生成预测，易违反传递性、对称性等关系约束，导致输出矛盾不可靠。

Method: 提出双轨机制：一为推理时方法，采用约束感知提示、基于约束的验证和迭代自省，无需微调即可改进黑盒LLM输出；二为训练时方法，通过知识蒸馏与强化学习（GRPO）将教师模型的推理轨迹迁移到学生模型，联合优化提取性能与关系一致性。

Result: 在DocRED数据集上，两个轨道均优于基线，推理时方法使用现成的黑盒模型即达竞争性F1，训练时方法显著缩小7-8B开源模型与顶尖专有模型差距，且推理成本极低。消融实验表明显式建模一致性有效减少关系矛盾，提升可靠性。

Conclusion: CONSISTRE框架统一处理了可访问API与本地部署场景下的关系一致性问题，显著提升了基于大语言模型的文档级关系抽取的可靠性和一致性。

Abstract: Document-level relation extraction (DocRE) aims to extract relations among multiple entities across extended contexts while maintaining consistency across predicted triples. Although large language models (LLMs) show remarkable reasoning capabilities in information extraction, their predictions are typically generated independently for each candidate triple and may violate fundamental relational constraints such as transitivity, symmetry, and functional uniqueness, leading to contradictory and unreliable outputs. We propose CONSISTRE, a unified consistency-aware framework for DocRE that addresses this limitation through two complementary tracks. The first operates at inference time for black-box LLMs, combining constraint-aware prompting, constraint-based verification, and iterative self-reflection to refine predictions without task-specific fine-tuning. The second injects consistency knowledge into smaller open-source models via a knowledge distillation and reinforcement learning pipeline: reasoning traces from a powerful teacher are distilled into a student via supervised fine-tuning, followed by GRPO alignment using a composite reward that jointly optimizes extraction performance and relational consistency. Together, the two tracks cover both API-accessible and locally deployable scenarios under a unified consistency formulation. Experiments on DocRED show that both tracks outperform their baselines, with the inference-time track achieving competitive F1 using off-the-shelf black-box LLMs and the training-time track substantially narrowing the gap between 7--8B open-source models and state-of-the-art proprietary LLMs at a fraction of their inference cost. Ablation studies confirm that explicit consistency modeling mitigates relational contradictions and enhances the reliability of LLM-based DocRE across both deployment paradigms.

</details>


### [130] [Cross-Attention Calibrated Deduplication for Retrieval-Augmented Generation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24332)
*Phuong Le Huy,Nam H. Nguyen,Quan V. Dang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种名为交叉注意力校准去重（CACD）的新方法，用于在RAG系统中更高效地去除冗余文本块。与传统基于余弦相似度的向量方法不同，CACD使用交叉编码器保留细粒度的标记级信息，并结合新信息得分（NIS）和多数投票机制，显著提升去重效果和处理速度。实验表明，CACD平均可移除9.75%的块，比现有方法更快，尤其远快于余弦相似度过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统中的常见分块策略会产生大量冗余块，导致向量数据库膨胀并降低检索效率。传统的余弦相似度阈值法因依赖单一向量表示，会丢失关键的标记级细节，难以准确区分真正重复与仅主题相似的内容。因此需要一种能保留细粒度语义信息、同时高效去重的方法。

Method: CACD采用交叉编码器对新块与内存中已保留块进行逐对比较，保留完整的标记级上下文信息；引入新信息得分（NIS），基于交叉编码器的注意力熵衡量未被已有块解释的部分；通过多个候选块的多数投票机制增强判断鲁棒性。

Result: 在SQuAD 1.1验证集上，CACD平均移除9.75%的文本块，接近其他语义级方法水平，远高于精确匹配过滤；处理速度平均为51.0秒/配置，比最强基线NERExact快27%，比余弦相似度过滤快约7倍。

Conclusion: CACD通过保留细粒度语义信息和多维度评估机制，在保持高去重率的同时显著提升处理效率，是一种有前景的RAG系统冗余块处理方案，但其效果仍需在更多数据集上验证。

Abstract: Common chunking strategies in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems often create redundant chunks. These redundant chunks make the vector database bigger and slow down retrieval. A common fix is cosine-similarity thresholding. This method reduces each chunk to a single vector, then compares vectors using a similarity score. But a single vector can lose the fine-grained, token-level detail needed to tell a true duplicate apart from a chunk that just shares the same topic. We propose Cross-Attention Calibrated Deduplication (CACD). CACD checks each new chunk against an in-memory pool of chunks already kept, using a cross-encoder instead of a single pooled vector. This keeps token-level detail all the way to the final comparison. CACD combines three parts: the cross-encoder comparison itself, a New Information Score (NIS) that measures how much of a chunk is not explained by a candidate already kept, and a majority vote across several candidates rather than a single best match. NIS is calculated from the attention entropy of the cross-encoder. We tested CACD against five existing filtering methods, nine chunking strategies, and 18 configurations, all on the full SQuAD 1.1 validation set. In our experiments, CACD removes 9.75% of chunks on average. This drop rate is close to other semantic-level methods, and much higher than exact-match filters, which barely remove anything. In these experiments, CACD also processes each configuration in 51.0 seconds on average, about 27% faster than the strongest baseline, NERExact (69.6s), and about 7x faster than cosine-similarity filtering (356.7s). These results come from a single dataset, so we present them as an early comparison, not a general claim. Code for the baseline evaluation and for CACD is available at https://github.com/lehuyphuong/rag_bench and https://github.com/lehuyphuong/cacd_dedup.

</details>


### [131] [Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models as Components of Cognitive Computing architecture for Regulatory Knowledge Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24352)
*Dariusz Nowak-Nova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了将大语言模型（LLM）与检索增强生成（RAG）架构结合，能否使其从独立生成模型转变为具备更高认知可靠性、可审计和可更新的智能计算基础设施组件。研究提出一种基于本地部署、无需高端GPU的LLM架构，适用于持续分析和解释法律文本的监管管理场景。通过整合本地LLM与外部知识库，构建混合认知系统，实现语义理解与受控知识检索、上下文关联及来源追溯。实验在Ollama和LM Studio环境中使用波兰语模型Bielik和PLLuM，在消费级硬件上完成，验证结果显示RAG显著提升生成内容的事实一致性、领域特异性与规范精确性，降低生成无依据内容的风险，并实现审计性、可控知识管理与动态更新能力。结论认为，增强RAG的本地化LLM不应仅视为文本生成工具，而应作为支持合规与决策的认知计算模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在生成法律或监管相关文本时存在事实不一致、缺乏可追溯性和难以动态更新等问题，尤其在高法律波动性环境中，其可靠性不足。为提升其在监管管理中的应用价值，亟需构建更具可信度与可控性的认知系统。因此，探索将本地部署的LLM与RAG结合，以增强其在真实业务场景中的实用性与安全性。

Method: 提出一种基于本地部署大语言模型的混合认知架构，采用非云端、低资源消耗的执行环境（如Ollama和LM Studio），结合波兰语模型Bielik和PLLuM，利用外部知识库实现检索增强。通过引入RAG机制，使语言模型在生成过程中依赖经过验证的知识源，实现上下文感知、来源追踪与知识更新，同时保持模型本身无需重新训练。

Result: 实验表明，集成RAG后，生成内容在事实一致性、领域适配性与法律规范准确性方面显著提升；减少了幻觉和无依据生成；实现了对信息来源的可审计性、知识库的动态更新能力，且无需重训练模型；系统可在消费级硬件上稳定运行，具备实际部署可行性。

Conclusion: 本地部署的大语言模型通过与RAG架构融合，可有效转化为支持监管合规与组织决策的认知计算核心模块，不仅提升了生成内容的可靠性与可审计性，还增强了系统的灵活性与适应性，适用于高信息波动与法律复杂性环境。

Abstract: The aim of this article is to verify whether integrating large language models (LLMs) with the Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) architecture enables their transformation from standalone generative models into components of cognitive computing infrastructure with enhanced epistemic reliability. The study proposes an architectural approach based on locally deployed LLMs operating in on-premises environments without high-end GPU accelerators and examines their applicability in supporting regulatory management processes requiring continuous analysis and interpretation of legal acts. The proposed solution combines local LLMs with external knowledge repositories, creating a hybrid cognitive architecture in which the language model performs semantic interpretation while the RAG layer provides controlled knowledge retrieval, contextualization, and traceability of information sources. The implementation was validated using the Ollama and LM Studio execution environments together with the Polish language models Bielik and PLLuM running on consumer-class hardware. The results demonstrate that augmenting LLMs with RAG significantly improves the factual consistency, domain specificity and normative precision of generated texts while reducing the risk of unsupported content generation. Furthermore, the study shows that integrating RAG introduces auditability, controlled knowledge management and dynamic updating of regulatory information without retraining the language model. The findings indicate that locally deployed LLMs enhanced with RAG should be regarded not merely as text generation tools but as semantic processing modules within cognitive computing infrastructures supporting regulatory compliance and organizational decision-making in environments characterized by high legal and informational volatility.

</details>


### [132] [Keep It InMind: Benchmarking the Implicit-Association Blind Spot in Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24368)
*Ruizhe Li,Mingxuan Du,Benfeng Xu,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出InMind基准，用于评估长期记忆系统在处理隐含关联查询时的性能。现有系统在间接查询上表现差，尽管能准确召回事实，问题出在查询条件下的接口设计，而非记忆存储或检索能力。通过诊断探针验证，保持记忆可见性可显著提升性能，表明路由机制是核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆系统假设记忆与查询之间存在显式匹配，但现实世界中许多关联是隐含的（如坚果过敏与杏仁粉成分），导致系统无法正确推理。因此需要一个能区分记忆是否存储、是否具备桥接知识、是否被成功调用的基准测试来揭示真实瓶颈。

Method: 构建InMind基准，包含125个任务，覆盖十个生活领域，113个任务基于可验证公开来源；使用配对控制实验分离三种可能解释（未存储、缺乏桥接知识、未浮现）；对比六种记忆系统在有无上下文记忆可见性条件下的表现，并引入高维嵌入和最小诊断探针进行分析。

Result: 骨干模型在提供上下文记忆时可回答84.0%的间接查询；其他六种记忆系统最多仅达到14.4%，即使它们在直接召回事实时可达100%；增加嵌入维度无法缩小差距；而保持记忆可见性的探针几乎恢复了性能差距，证明失败源于查询条件下的接口设计。

Conclusion: 当前记忆系统的瓶颈不在于记忆存储或检索能力，而在于查询条件下的记忆路由机制。InMind为评估这一关键问题提供了可靠基准，指明未来研究应聚焦于如何动态决定哪些记忆必须保持可见。

Abstract: Long-term memory systems store what a user says in an external store and retrieve it when a related query arrives. This interface rests on an assumption so natural that it is rarely stated: a memory that is needed will resemble the query that needs it. World knowledge breaks the assumption. A tree-nut allergy should change the answer to a macaron request through their almond-flour ingredient, yet the two texts share no cue a retriever can see. We call this failure mode the implicit-association blind spot and introduce InMind, a 125-task, expert-verified benchmark spanning ten life domains, with 113 tasks grounded in citable public sources. Its paired controls separate three explanations that existing evaluations conflate: the fact was never stored, the model lacks the bridging knowledge, or the fact was stored and never surfaced. The verdict is clean. With the decisive memory placed in context, the backbone answers 84.0 percent of indirect queries; when the same memory must be retrieved, six vector, graph, and agentic memory systems reach at most 14.4 percent, even though they recall the same facts on demand at up to 100 percent. An embedding with eight times the dimensionality raises answer-blind target recall for every system yet leaves the gap essentially intact. A minimal diagnostic probe that keeps memory visible before the query arrives recovers most of the gap, locating the failure in the query-conditioned interface itself and pointing to routing, deciding which facts must stay visible, as the open problem InMind is built to score.

</details>


### [133] [Closed-Loop Validation-Repair for Healthcare Interoperability: A Multi-Model Study of Schema Compliance in Clinical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24371)
*Jianru Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了三种开源大模型（Qwen2.5 7B、Llama 3.1 8B、Gemma2 9B）在320个临床场景下的结构化输出合规性，发现其基础合规率在85.9%至91.6%之间，且主要问题为表示层面的格式错误（如缩写、代码前缀不一致）。通过引入验证-修复框架，整体合规率提升至99.0%，误差多在1-2次迭代内解决，统计显著性高（p<0.001），表明闭环验证-修复机制可有效保障医疗互操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型虽具临床推理能力，但在电子病历系统集成中面临结构化输出不符合医疗标准（如ICD-10、CPT、HL7 FHIR）的障碍，亟需系统级方法保障输出合规性以实现真实世界应用。

Method: 采用本地部署方式，在十大学科共320个临床场景下测试三个模型，生成960个模型-场景组合；对比基线与验证-修复条件下的输出合规性；使用自动化验证器识别格式错误，并通过迭代反馈进行修复。

Result: 基线合规率85.9%-91.6%；96%的错误为表示层格式问题；验证-修复框架使合规率提升至99.0%（98.4%-99.4%），平均改进7.8-12.5个百分点，且多数错误在1-2轮内修复，统计显著（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 闭环验证-修复机制是保障大模型输出符合医疗数据标准的有效系统级策略，显著提升其在临床系统集成中的结构化准备度，推动医疗人工智能向实际互操作性落地迈进。

Abstract: Healthcare interoperability requires AI systems to produce structured outputs conforming to standardized schemas including ICD-10 for diagnostic coding, CPT for procedure billing, and HL7 FHIR for data exchange. While large language models demonstrate clinical reasoning capabilities, their integration into electronic health record systems faces a critical barrier: schema noncompliance. We evaluate three open-source models, Qwen2.5 7B, Llama 3.1 8B, and Gemma2 9B, via local deployment across 320 clinical scenarios spanning ten medical specialties, yielding 960 model-scenario pairs assessed under paired baseline and validation-repair conditions. First, schema noncompliance is consistent across the three model families, with baseline compliance rates ranging from 85.9 to 91.6 percent despite varying architectures and training data, suggesting shared gaps in medical training corpora rather than model-specific limitations. Second, 96 percent of validator-detected failures are representation-level format violations such as alternative medical abbreviations and code prefixes, indicating models follow clinical writing conventions but lack awareness of healthcare IT standards. Third, the validation-repair framework achieves 99.0 percent overall compliance, ranging from 98.4 to 99.4 percent across models, with most errors resolving within one or two iterations. Exact McNemar p-values below 0.001 and absolute improvements of 7.8 to 12.5 percentage points across model sizes confirm statistical significance. These results support closed-loop validation-repair as an effective system-level safeguard for healthcare interoperability, improving schema-level readiness for downstream clinical system integration.

</details>


### [134] [LEX-EC: A Lexical Evidence-Channel Audit Framework for Zero-Shot LLM Personality Classification in Black-Box Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24435)
*Brittany Harbison,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LEX-EC框架，通过结合流行度与一致性诊断及受控词汇消解，区分边际分布效应与特定人格特质信号，揭示不同文本类型中人格标签的可识别性差异，强调语言特征在黑箱模型解释中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽能轻易分配人格标签，但其可解释性仍是开放问题，亟需一种方法来区分真实特质信号与表面相关性。

Method: 引入LEX-EC框架，整合流行度、一致性、词汇消解、功能词与情感词分析，评估模型在不同文本类型下的人格推断稳定性与可解释性。

Result: 发现自由写作文本信号最广但弱，研究生简介中外向性关联随掩码减弱，单条社交状态几乎无稳定证据；功能词、情感词和认知风格词汇仍保留部分关联，提示语言提示可影响自解释但无法消除主题内容影响。

Conclusion: LEX-EC有效刻画了人格关联随词汇证据变化的动态特性，为黑箱模型在人格标注中的可解释性提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models may easily assign personality labels from text, but model interpretability remains an open problem. To address this gap, we introduce LEX-EC, a reusable black-box audit framework combining prevalence and agreement diagnostics with controlled lexical ablation to distinguish marginal-distribution effects from trait-associated signal recoverable under restricted evidence. Using this framework, we illustrate how various text genres may exhibit sharply different profiles: free-form essay text contains the broadest, but still weak, signal; in graduate student introductions, an observable Extraversion association weakened after masking; and single Facebook statuses yield little stable evidence even in a trait-balanced sample, indicating a possible lower bound of content or length. Masking topical and demographic content weakened some associations while leaving others detectable from function words, affective terms, and cognitive-style vocabulary. Linguistic prompting shifted model self-explanations but did not eliminate topical content. LEX-EC jointly evaluates classification prevalence, item-level association, chance-corrected agreement, persistence under lexical restriction, and prompt sensitivity in model-generated explanations. Across datasets, models, and prompts, LEX-EC characterizes how trait associations may vary with available lexical evidence, introducing a novel application of lexical methods to black-box interpretability in personality labeling.

</details>


### [135] [Grounding latent algorithm routing in transformer reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24471)
*Xiangbo Zhang,Xiaoxu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入ROUTEBENCH基准，探究Transformer模型在固定提示形式下能否根据潜在数据生成机制动态调整归纳偏置偏好。实验表明，306M参数的密集解码器模型可弥补80.9%的最优路由差距，路由F1达84.1，在多种干扰条件下保持稳定，且可通过特定激活干预调控。内部探测与激活替换控制验证了路由相关内部方向的存在与功能作用。结果表明，训练后的Transformer能发展出类似路由的内部变量，但不支持通用路由或自然语言推理中的广泛适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer是否能在固定提示下，依据潜在数据生成机制动态调整归纳偏置偏好，以理解其在上下文学习中的适应能力。

Method: 采用潜算法路由（latent algorithm routing）设计，构建ROUTEBENCH基准，包含全局收缩、稀疏性、鲁棒性和局部性四种不同归纳偏置家族的代表性任务；使用从头训练的密集解码器变压器（44M-612M参数），评估路由性能，并通过自然语言渲染、支持集打乱、词汇改写和统一四路路由设置测试鲁棒性；同时对比输入条件软混合、无监督Gumbel路由等替代方法，并进行探针控制与激活修补控制验证内部机制。

Result: 306M模型在路由F1上达到84.1，弥补80.9%的最优路由差距；在各种扰动下表现稳定；更强的替代方法虽有改进但仍低于306M和612M模型；内部方向可解码且与输出行为一致，表明存在功能性的路由机制。

Conclusion: 密集型Transformer在特定训练设置下可发展出类似路由的内部变量，具备对不同归纳偏置家族的自适应能力，但这一能力尚未扩展至预训练语言模型或广泛的自然语言推理任务中。

Abstract: A central question in the in-context learning literature is whether transformers can organize episode-level adaptation around different inductive-bias families. We study this question in a controlled setting through latent algorithm routing: route-like behavior in which the solver-family preference changes with the latent data-generating regime while prompt form is held fixed, remains stable under nuisance perturbations, and is selectively influenced by targeted activation interventions without large losses in answer quality. We introduce ROUTEBENCH, a diagnostic benchmark whose regimes differentially favor global shrinkage, sparsity, robustness, and locality, operationalized by ridge-like, lasso-like, Huber-like, and kNN-like family representatives. Across dense decoder-only transformers trained from scratch at 44M-612M parameters, a 306M model closes 80.9 percent of the oracle-routing gap and achieves route F1 of 84.1. The effect remains substantial under natural-language renderings, shuffled supports, lexical paraphrases, and a unified four-way routing setting. Stronger adaptive alternatives, including an input-conditioned soft mixture and an unsupervised Gumbel router, narrow the gap but remain below the 306M and 612M models on route F1 and OOD performance. Probe controls and matched activation-patching controls further show that route-relevant internal directions are decodable and functionally involved in solver-family-consistent output behavior. These results provide controlled evidence that dense transformers trained on ROUTEBENCH can develop route-like internal variables, but they do not establish universal routing in pretrained language models or unrestricted natural-language reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Concept-based Visual Counterfactual Explanations with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22544)
*Yassine Oueslati,Daniil Kirilenko,Martin Gjoreski,Marc Langheinrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: C-VCE 提出一种新的扩散框架，将分类器嵌入生成模型中，通过概念瓶颈层实现对可解释语义概念的控制，从而生成更真实、更可控的视觉反事实解释。相比依赖外部噪声鲁棒分类器的方法，C-VCE 不依赖额外分类器，能更精准地调整图像相关区域，保持整体一致性，并在保持低扰动的同时有效改变模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的视觉反事实解释方法依赖外部噪声鲁棒分类器，但在处理噪声图像时表现脆弱，难以部署。为提升解释的可靠性与实用性，需构建一个内置分类能力且可解释的生成框架。

Method: 引入 C-VCE 框架，通过在扩散模型中加入概念瓶颈层，将分类任务与生成过程融合；用户可开关语义概念以控制编辑方向；结合概率正则化项和梯度掩码，确保修改小而集中，同时保持原始图像结构。

Result: 在 CelebA 等基准上，C-VCE 在保持或提升预测翻转率的同时，生成的反事实图像更接近原图，失真更少，视觉质量更高，且无需依赖外部分类器，具备更强的鲁棒性与可解释性。

Conclusion: 将内部概念层显式暴露并可控制，是提升生成模型可解释性与安全性的重要路径。C-VCE 为安全关键领域中的视觉系统提供了实用、可信的反事实解释工具。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations aim to answer "what minimal change to this image would flip the model's prediction?", and are increasingly important as vision models are deployed in safety-critical domains (e.g., medicine). Existing diffusion-based methods can produce realistic edits, but they rely on external classifiers that must work reliably on noisy images, which makes them fragile and hard to deploy for robust explanations. We introduce C-VCE, a new diffusion framework that builds the classifier directly into the generative model via a concept bottleneck layer, so that counterfactuals are guided by human-interpretable features (concepts) instead of a separate noise robust classifier that works with pixel-level edits. Our model lets users to toggle on/off semantic concepts during sampling, then minimally adjusts relevant image regions, while preserving the rest of the image, respecting feature correlations. To keep edits small and controlled, we add a simple probabilistic regularizer that balances "change the prediction" against "stay close to the original", plus a gradient-based mask that confines modifications to the most relevant regions. On benchmarks such as CelebA, C-VCE matches or improves flip rates while producing counterfactuals that are visually closer to the input and less distorted than baselines that depend on separate noisy-image classifiers. These properties make C-VCE a practical tool for vision systems where users need concrete "what-if" images without having to trust an additional, noise-robust classifier. More broadly, our results suggest that exposing and controlling an internal concept layer is a promising way to make powerful generative models easier to understand and safer to use.

</details>


### [137] [SeT-Diff: Towards Semantic Foundation Models for HPC Telemetry and Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22548)
*Giovanni B. Esposito,Francesco Antici,Daniele Cesarini,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SeT-Diff 是首个用于计算节点遥测和时间序列的基础模型，采用基于扩散的方法，通过传感器的语义描述条件化生成过程，实现系统动态与数据集结构的解耦。该模型在真实超算数据集上表现出色，重建任务的平均绝对误差（MAE）仅为0.0470，具备零样本排列稳定性，且在传感器顺序变化时性能几乎不变。单一预训练模型可同时完成数据插补、预测和虚拟传感，热推断任务中达到0.033的MAE，展现出强大的通用性和实用性，是高性能计算系统中高效的数据驱动数字孪生方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法在高绩效计算（HPC）系统中依赖静态、固定位置的匿名传感器变量，仅适用于特定任务，当任务或传感器配置改变时模型迅速失效。亟需一种灵活、通用且能适应动态环境的数字孪生建模方法，以应对复杂工作负载、环境参数与物理指标之间的相互作用。

Method: 提出 SeT-Diff，一种基于扩散模型的通用计算节点遥测建模方法。其核心在于将生成过程条件化于每个传感器的语义描述，从而实现系统动态与数据结构的解耦。该设计使模型不依赖于传感器的固定顺序或具体类型，具备高度灵活性与泛化能力。

Result: 在真实超算数据集上的实验表明，SeT-Diff 在重建任务中达到 0.0470 的 MAE；在传感器随机排列的情况下仍保持稳定性能，体现零样本排列稳定性；单个预训练模型可有效执行数据插补、预测与虚拟传感，热推断任务中 MAE 达到 0.033，显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: SeT-Diff 作为首个面向计算节点遥测的时间序列基础模型，突破了传统方法对固定传感器配置的依赖，实现了跨任务、跨配置的通用建模能力。其卓越的泛化性、稳定性与多功能性，使其成为构建高性能计算系统高效数字孪生的理想选择。

Abstract: Data centers and their compute nodes require accurate and flexible digital twins capable of modeling the complex interplay of workloads, environmental parameters, and physical metrics. Current machine learning approaches for HPC and its telemetry typically rely on a static subset of anonymous, fixed-position sensor variables tailored to single tasks. Consequently, these models become obsolete when target tasks change or sensor metrics vary. We propose SeT-Diff, the first foundational model for compute node telemetry and time-series. Unlike rigid architectures, our diffusion-based approach conditions the generative process on each sensor's semantic description, decoupling the system dynamics from the structure of the dataset. Experiments on a real-world supercomputer dataset demonstrate a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.0470 on reconstruction tasks. SeT-Diff exhibits zero-shot permutation stability, maintaining accuracy with negligible degradation even when sensors are shuffled. A single pre-trained model effectively performs data imputation, forecasting, and virtual sensing - achieving a 0.033 MAE in thermal inference - making SeT-Diff an effective data-driven digital twin for HPC systems.

</details>


### [138] [QFoldAgent: An Autonomous Quantum Optimization Multi-Agent System for Protein Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22549)
*Winson Chen,Yuqi Zhang,Sixu Chen,Nuo Xu,Qiang Guan,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QFoldAgent 是一个闭环多智能体框架，用于5残基四面体晶格蛋白质折叠，通过设计、量子-经典优化和反馈智能体迭代优化哈密顿量惩罚权重，在不使用真实结构信息的情况下显著提升折叠精度与结构有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合量子-经典蛋白质结构预测方法依赖手动设定的哈密顿量惩罚权重，且仅在短片段上测试，限制了性能提升；本文旨在通过闭环多智能体系统自动优化惩罚权重，提高折叠精度与结构可靠性。

Method: 提出QFoldAgent框架，包含三个智能体：设计智能体生成序列条件惩罚，基于VQE的量子-经典管道在Qiskit Aer噪声下优化哈密顿量，反馈智能体利用能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代调整惩罚。整个过程不暴露真实结构指标，仅用于评估。

Result: 在QDockBank数据集上，中位RMSD从3.64 Å降至3.20 Å，尤其在困难目标上改善明显；在未见序列上，结构有效性从87.5%提升至98.7%，87%的无效结构被恢复，强控制器在87%序列中改善第3轮能量，同时保持96%的主链二面角合理性。

Conclusion: 迭代式智能体控制可系统性改进5残基量子设置下的优化行为，减少失败案例，为未来量子蛋白质折叠提供有效范式。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical protein structure prediction depends strongly on Hamiltonian penalty weights, yet existing lattice-based workflows typically fix these coefficients by hand and evaluate only very short fragments in simulation. We present QFoldAgent, a closed-loop multi-agent framework for 5-residue tetrahedral-lattice folding in which a design agent proposes sequence-conditioned penalties, a VQE-based quantum-classical pipeline optimizes the resulting Hamiltonian under Qiskit Aer noise, and a feedback agent uses energy-landscape diagnostics and MolProbity validation signals to refine penalties across cycles. Ground-truth metrics such as RMSD are never exposed to the agents and are used only for evaluation. We study the framework on two complementary datasets: 55 QDockBank-derived fragments with known structures and 100 coverage-optimized unseen sequences. On the QDockBank benchmark, QFoldAgent reduces median RMSD from 3.64 Å to 3.20 Å, with the largest gains on the hardest targets. On unseen sequences, the closed loop raises structural validity from 87.5% to 98.7%, recovers 87% of initially invalid cases, and the strongest controller improves cycle-3 energy on 87% of sequences while maintaining 96% Ramachandran-favored geometry. These results show that iterative agent control can systematically improve optimization behavior and reduce failure cases in a 5-residue quantum setting.

</details>


### [139] [Same Question, Different Answers: Evaluating LLM Reliability Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22554)
*Kazem Faghih,Yize Cheng,Shoumik Saha,Mobina Pournemat,Armin Gerami,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在面对语义相同但表述不同的问题时的稳定性，发现尽管整体准确率变化不大，但具体到单个问题，模型答案频繁因措辞不同而波动，错误翻转率超过23%。即使在原始问题正确回答的情况下，也存在大量答案翻转现象，表明单一提示下的正确性不能反映模型可靠性。然而，多数问题至少有一个改写版本能被正确回答，说明知识存在但检索不一致。通过简单的自改写策略可部分恢复潜在知识，提升推理表现。因此，标准准确率可能掩盖了模型的不稳定性，一致性评估更能揭示模型的真实可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在面对语义等价但表述不同的问题时的稳定性，揭示当前准确率指标可能掩盖的模型不可靠性问题。

Method: 在四个基准测试和13个模型上，对事实问答与数学推理任务进行意义保持的改写（paraphrase）实验，分析模型在不同表述下的输出变化，计算答案翻转率，并引入自改写策略以验证知识恢复潜力。

Result: 模型在不同表述下答案稳定性差，实例级不一致性显著，答案翻转率超过23%；即便原始问题答对，仍存在高翻转率；自改写策略可有效提升性能，说明知识虽存在但检索不一致。

Conclusion: 标准准确率指标可能掩盖大语言模型在语义等价输入下的严重不稳定性。评估模型在不同表述间的一致性，是更可靠的可靠性衡量方式；通过自改写等方法可部分恢复潜藏知识，提升实际应用中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often achieve strong accuracy on benchmarks, yet it remains unclear how reliably they apply this knowledge when the same question is phrased in different but equivalent ways. In this work, we study how model answers change under meaning-preserving paraphrases across factual question answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across four benchmarks and 13 models, we find that model outputs frequently depend on the exact wording of the prompt. While overall accuracy typically changes only modestly across paraphrases, instance-level behavior is far less stable: for many questions, models alternate between correct and incorrect answers depending on phrasing, with mismatch rates reaching more than 23%. Conditioning on questions that are answered correctly in their original form reveals even larger failures measured by answer flip rates, showing that single-prompt correctness is often a poor indicator of reliability. At the same time, we find that models often produce a correct answer for at least one paraphrase of a question, suggesting that the underlying knowledge is present but inconsistently retrieved. Building on this observation, we show that a simple self-paraphrasing strategy can partially recover this latent knowledge and improve performance at inference time. Together, these findings suggest that standard accuracy metrics can mask substantial instability, and that evaluating consistency across equivalent inputs provides a clearer picture of LLM reliability.

</details>


### [140] [DeepLens Diagnosis Agent: Agentic Workflow Design Lets a Small Reasoning Model Compete with Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22555)
*Mahmood Bayeshi,Veysel Kocaman,Muhammed Ali Naqvi,Yigit Gul,David Talby*

Main category: cs.AI
